# GOLDENSYNC: SYSTEM PROGRESS TRACKER

> Live monitoring ledger for the Figma prototype build described in `figma-prototype-system-plan.md`.
> The build agent (Cursor) is also the **monitoring agent**: it updates this file after **every** change to the Figma file.
> If this file and the Figma file disagree, the Figma file is the truth and the disagreement must be logged as `DRIFT` (section 11).

## 1. MONITORING PROTOCOL (THE AGENT MUST FOLLOW EVERY RULE)

```
R1  READ FIRST   At session start: read this file, then read the live Figma file state
                 (pages, frame list, variable defs). Compare. Log any mismatch as DRIFT (section 11).
R2  LOG EVERY    After every use_figma batch that changes the file, append ONE entry to the Change Log
    CHANGE       (section 9) BEFORE starting the next batch. No batching of log entries.
R3  APPEND-ONLY  Never edit or delete past Change Log, Verification, Audit, Decision or Drift entries.
                 A wrong entry is corrected by a new entry of type CORRECTION that references it.
R4  STATUS RULES Status moves only forward: TODO > WIP > BUILT > VERIFIED. Exceptions: BLOCKED (any time),
                 REWORK (VERIFIED/BUILT > WIP, needs a Change Log entry giving the reason).
R5  NO PROOF,    An item is VERIFIED only when a screenshot was taken AFTER its last change and compared
    NO VERIFIED  to the plan. Put the Verification ID (VER-nnnn) in the item row.
R6  IDS          Record the Figma node ID for every frame, component, variable collection and flow.
                 Without a node ID an item cannot be BUILT.
R7  RECOMPUTE    After every update, recompute the Dashboard (section 2) counts and the page snapshot (section 3).
R8  ONE TRUTH    Do not copy values (colors, sizes) into this file. Reference the plan section instead.
R9  HONESTY      Never mark work done that was skipped, approximated, or failed. Use SKIPPED or BLOCKED and say why.
R10 STOP RULE    If the same item fails 3 times, mark BLOCKED, log it in the Blocker Register (section 12), and move on.
R11 MILESTONE    A milestone closes only when every item in it is VERIFIED or SKIPPED (with reason), its audit
    GATE         passed (section 14), and the milestone row in section 2 is updated.
R12 PLAN CHANGES Any change to scope, copy, or tokens that differs from the plan is logged in section 10
                 (Decision Log) with the reason. Do not silently deviate.
```

### 1.1 Status codes

| Code       | Meaning |
| ---------- | --- |
| `TODO`     | Not started |
| `WIP`      | In progress this session |
| `BUILT`    | Created in Figma, node ID recorded, not yet verified |
| `VERIFIED` | Screenshot checked against the plan, verification ID recorded |
| `REWORK`   | Previously BUILT or VERIFIED, reopened. Reason in Change Log |
| `BLOCKED`  | Cannot proceed. Entry in Blocker Register |
| `SKIPPED`  | Deliberately not built. Reason in Decision Log |
| `N/A`      | Not applicable (for example, a stretch item on a required list) |

### 1.2 ID formats

`CHG-0001` change · `VER-0001` verification · `AUD-0001` audit · `DEC-001` decision · `DRF-001` drift · `BLK-001` blocker · `SES-001` session

### 1.3 Change entry types

`CREATE` · `UPDATE` · `DELETE` · `RENAME` · `BIND` (variable binding) · `WIRE` (prototype interaction) · `REFACTOR` · `FIX` · `CORRECTION` · `SYNC` (tracker-only update)

- `DEC-002 · 2026-11-10 · The existing context/system-progress-tracker.md is the tracker (extended with 15A, 15B, 18) instead of creating a second file in the repo root · avoids two diverging trackers · rejected: new root file · master prompt 5.4 · build agent`
- `DEC-003 · 2026-11-10 · Page-panel screenshots (piece 0.3) are replaced by the API page list · the MCP renders nodes, not Figma UI chrome · Known limit · build agent`
- `DEC-004 · 2026-11-10 · Piece 1.4 builds the plan 5.2 tint list (8 tints + 4 glow/glass = 12) and adds one token, tint-error-hover (red 20%) · piece 1.4 verify line ("12 tint + 4 glow") disagrees with plan 5.2 (12 total); plan is the spec. DESIGN_RULES 7.1 gives Destructive hover rgba(248,113,113,0.20), which needs a token to stay bound · rejected: inventing 4 unnamed tints to hit 16 · plan 5.2, DESIGN_RULES 7.1 · build agent`
- `DEC-005 · 2026-11-10 · White-alpha and tint tokens hold raw rgba values, not aliases · Figma aliases cannot change alpha; plan 5.1 allows "an alias to a primitive or an alpha value" · build agent`
- `DEC-006 · 2026-11-10 · Two text styles added beyond the 16: body-md-medium (16/500/160%) and body-sm-medium (14/500/150%) · DESIGN_RULES 7.1 (button label body-md weight 500) and 7.2 (label body-sm weight 500) require weight 500 at those sizes, and every text node must carry a named style (plan 13.1) · rejected: overriding weight on the node (breaks the style link) · DESIGN_RULES 4.1/7.1/7.2, plan 5.3 · build agent`
- `DEC-007 · 2026-11-10 · Glass styles are split: the effect style carries blur + shadows; the fill and 1px stroke from plan 5.4 are bound on each node to Semantic variables (named in the style description) · Figma effect styles cannot store fills or strokes · rejected: paint styles per glass fill (would duplicate tokens) · plan 5.4 · build agent`
- `KL-01 · Known limit · Inter tabular figures (OpenType tnum) cannot be set on a Figma TextStyle through the Plugin API. Numbers in tables use mono-md/mono-sm (Roboto Mono, fixed-width digits) per DESIGN_RULES 4.2 "Monospace for scores", so columns still align`
- `DEC-008 · 2026-11-10 · Button horizontal padding sm 12 / md 16 / lg 24 (space-3/4/6), icon gap 8 (space-2); heights fixed at the spec values 36/44/52 · DESIGN_RULES 7.1 and ui-context 8.1 give heights but no padding; 36/44/52 cannot be built from 4-based padding + line height · rejected: padding-derived heights (would change the spec heights) · DESIGN_RULES 7.1 · build agent`
- `DEC-009 · 2026-11-10 · Button hover "brightness 110%" drawn as a second fill surface-glass-lg (white 10%) over the variant fill; hover scale 1.01 and press scale 0.98 not drawn (Active = Default visuals), noted in the component description · Figma has no brightness filter and scaled variants would give non-4 sizes · rejected: new hover tokens (not in plan 5.2) · DESIGN_RULES 7.1, plan 10 · build agent`

## 15A. BUILD PLAN (piece 0.1, max 15 lines)

1. File: `GoldenSync Prototype v1` (Figma Design), on the plan chosen in Q5.
2. Pages (plan 4.1): 00 Cover, 01 Tokens, 02 Components, 03 Auth, 04 Dashboard + Add Subject, 05 Subject Modules, 06 Grade Calculator, 07 Prototype Controls, 99 Scratch.
3. Order: strictly `piece-by-piece-goldensync.md`, one piece at a time, M0 → M10.
4. Tokens first (Primitives → Semantic → Space → Radius → Proto → text, effect, paint styles), then core components, then domain components.
5. Screens per page, desktop 1280 → tablet 768 → mobile 375; wiring after each page's frames exist; motion last (M10).
6. Every piece: re-read plan sections, build, screenshot, verify, fix, record ids in section 18 below.
7. GATE pieces run the plan 13.1 audit (all counts 0). REVIEW pieces (3.8, 6.17, 7.11, 10.9) use an independent subagent.
8. Precedence: draw.io > DESIGN_RULES > plan > ui-context. Plan section 2 decisions applied as written; everything else logged in 10.3.
9. `system-ai-workflow-rules.md` is not used (superseded).

## 17. CODE PROJECT SETUP LEDGER (APPEND-ONLY)

Tracks the Next.js codebase, separately from the Figma build above.
Setup entries use the ID format `SET-001`.

### 17.1 Current state

- **App folder:** `goldensync-web/`
  - This is the only npm project in the repo.
- **How to run locally:**
  1. Open a new terminal (it starts in `goldensync-web/` automatically).
  2. Run `npm run dev`.
  3. Open http://localhost:3000
- **How to verify:**
  - `npm run lint` passes.
  - `npm run build` passes.
  - Last checked: 2026-11-10 20:30
- **Pages built:**
  - Starter page only (`app/page.tsx`).
  - No GoldenSync screens yet.
- **Runtime:** Node 24.16.0, npm 11.13.0
- **Repo location:** USB flash drive (`E:`)
  - This makes installs very slow (2 to 15 minutes each).

### 17.2 Stack (installed versions)

- **Framework:** `next` 16.4.6 (App Router, Turbopack)
  - Status: configured.
  - `cacheComponents` and `partialPrefetching` are on in `next.config.ts`.
- **UI runtime:** `react` and `react-dom` 19.3.0
  - Status: done.
- **Language:** `typescript` 5.9.3
  - Status: done.
- **Styling:** `tailwindcss` 4.3.3, `@tailwindcss/turbopack`, `tw-animate-css`
  - Status: done.
  - The theme lives in `app/globals.css`.
- **Component kit:** `shadcn` 4.23.0 (style `base-nova`, built on `@base-ui/react`)
  - Helpers: `class-variance-authority`, `clsx`, `tailwind-merge`
  - Status: done.
  - `components.json` is present.
  - The `cn()` helper is in `lib/utils.ts`.
- **Icons:** `lucide-react` 1.71, `react-bootstrap-icons` 1.11
  - Status: installed.
- **Animation:** `motion` 14.12, `animejs` 4.5
  - Status: installed.
- **Auth:** `@clerk/nextjs` 7.9.25
  - Status: installed, **NOT configured**.
  - Missing: API keys, provider, route protection, sign-in pages.
- **Database:** `prisma` CLI 8.0.0-rc.33
  - Status: CLI only.
  - **No client, schema or database yet** (see SET-010).
- **Forms and validation:** `react-hook-form` 7.91, `zod` 4.6, `@hookform/resolvers` 7.0
  - Status: installed.
- **State:** `zustand` 5.1
  - Status: installed.
- **Tables:** `@tanstack/react-table` 9.3
  - Status: installed.
- **PDF upload and parsing:** `react-dropzone` 20.2, `pdf-parse` 2.4
  - Status: installed.
  - Rule: `pdf-parse` must only run in server code.
- **Toasts, dates, menus:** `sonner`, `date-fns`, `react-day-picker`, `cmdk`, `next-themes`
  - Status: installed.
- **Linting:** `eslint` 9.39.5, `eslint-config-next`, `eslint-plugin-jsx-a11y`
  - Status: done.
  - Uses the flat config file `eslint.config.mjs`.
- **Formatting:** `prettier` 3.9, `prettier-plugin-tailwindcss` 0.8
  - Status: installed.
  - No config file yet.
- **Performance audits:** `@lhci/cli` 0.16 (Lighthouse CI)
  - Status: installed.
  - No config file yet.

  - accordion, badge, button, calendar, card, checkbox

> Re-adding it with the shadcn CLI will overwrite that fix.

### 17.4 Setup log

All entries dated 2026-11-10.

#### SET-001 · Fixed ESLint conflict in the first app (`web/`)

- **What was done:**
  - Upgraded ESLint 8 to ESLint 9.
  - Replaced `.eslintrc.json` with `eslint.config.mjs`.
  - Changed the lint script from `next lint` to `eslint .`
- **Why:**
  - An earlier `npm audit fix --force` upgraded Next to 16.
  - Next 16 needs ESLint 9 and removed the `next lint` command.

#### SET-002 · Created the new app `goldensync-web/`

- **What was done:**
  - New app with Next 16, React 19 and Tailwind 4.
  - Set up shadcn (style `base-nova`) with 22 components.
- **Why:**
  - Replaced `web/`, which was on React 18 and Tailwind 3.

#### SET-003 · Added Magic UI components

- **What was done:**
  - Added 7 Magic UI components with the shadcn CLI.
  - Fixed lint errors in `particles.tsx`.
- **Why:**
  - The first attempt failed because it ran from the repo root instead of `goldensync-web/`.

#### SET-004 · Installed Clerk in the right folder

- **What was done:**
  - Installed `@clerk/nextjs` in `goldensync-web/`.
  - Removed it from the repo root.
- **Why:**
  - It was first installed at the root by mistake.
  - That pulled in a second copy of React, which can break the app.

#### SET-005 · Made `goldensync-web/` the only npm project

- **What was done:**
  - Moved the root dev tools into `goldensync-web/`:
    - `prisma`
    - `prettier`, `prettier-plugin-tailwindcss`
    - `eslint-plugin-jsx-a11y`
    - `@lhci/cli`
  - Moved the `postinstall` script too.
  - Deleted the root `package.json`, `package-lock.json` and `node_modules`.
- **Why:**
  - With one npm project, commands can't land in the wrong folder.
  - Also fixed Next's "multiple lockfiles" warning.

#### SET-006 · Terminals open in the app folder

- **What was done:**
  - Added `.vscode/settings.json` with `terminal.integrated.cwd` set to `goldensync-web`.
- **Why:**
  - New terminals open in the app folder, so plain `npm run dev` works.

#### SET-007 · Installed `pdf-parse`

- **What was done:**
  - Installed `pdf-parse` in `goldensync-web/`.
  - It was first installed at the root by mistake, then moved.
- **Why:**
  - Needed for syllabus PDF parsing.

#### SET-008 · Moved libraries over from `web/`

- **What was done:**
  - Installed in `goldensync-web/`:
    - `zustand`
    - `react-hook-form`, `zod`, `@hookform/resolvers`
    - `@tanstack/react-table`
    - `react-dropzone`
    - `animejs`
    - `react-bootstrap-icons`
  - Added `clsx` and `tailwind-merge`.
  - Removed `@types/pdf-parse` and `@types/animejs`.
- **Why:**
  - The removed type packages describe older major versions.
  - `pdf-parse` 2 and `animejs` 4 ship their own types.

#### SET-009 · Deleted the old `web/` folder

- **Why:**
  - It held only the starter template, no custom code.

#### SET-010 · Skipped two packages from `web/`

- **What was skipped:**
  - `next-auth`
  - `@prisma/client`
- **Why:**
  - Clerk replaces `next-auth`.
  - No `@prisma/client` 8 exists to match the Prisma 8 RC CLI.
  - `@prisma/client` 7.10.1 would mismatch the CLI.

#### SET-011 · Recovered from a stuck `npm audit fix`

- **What was done:**
  - Stopped `npm audit fix` after it looped for about 1 hour on Prisma 8 RC peer conflicts.
  - Restored `node_modules` with `npm install`.
  - Deleted leftover npm temp folders.
  - Confirmed lint and build pass.
- **Why:**
  - The interruption had removed the Prisma CLI from `node_modules`.

### 17.5 Known issues and rules

#### ISS-01 · 25 vulnerability warnings from `npm audit` · `accepted`

- **Details:** 2 low, 14 moderate, 9 high.
- **Rule:**
  - All are in local tools (shadcn CLI, ESLint, Lighthouse CI, Prisma CLI).
  - None are shipped to the browser.
  - No upstream fix exists yet.
  - **Never run `npm audit fix --force`.**

#### ISS-02 · `npm audit fix` hangs on Prisma 8 RC · `open`

- **Rule:**
  - Do not run `npm audit fix` while on Prisma 8 RC.

#### ISS-03 · Repo is on a USB flash drive · `open`

- **Effect:** installs take 2 to 15 minutes.
- **Fix:**
  1. Copy the repo to the internal NVMe drive, without `node_modules`.
  2. Run `npm install` in `goldensync-web/`.

#### ISS-04 · npm commands from the repo root fail · `resolved`

- **Effect:** error "no package.json".
- **Rule:**
  - Run npm commands from `goldensync-web/`.
  - New terminals start there (SET-006).

#### ISS-05 · Prisma skills copied twice · `accepted`

- **Details:**
  - `postinstall` (`prisma skills sync`) now runs from `goldensync-web/`.
  - It copies the Prisma skills into `goldensync-web/.claude`, `.cursor`, `.agents` and `.devin`.
  - These duplicate the copies at the repo root.
- **Rule:**
  - Harmless.
  - `prisma.config.ts` (skills config only) stays at the repo root.

#### ISS-06 · Unused `cn` package · `open`

- **Details:**
  - The `cn` npm package is in `dependencies` but unused.
  - The `cn()` helper comes from `lib/utils.ts`.
- **Fix:** safe to uninstall.

#### ISS-07 · `next build` rewrote `tsconfig.json` · `accepted`

- **Details:** set `jsx: react-jsx` and `target: ES2017`.
- **Rule:** expected for Next 16. Keep and commit.

### 17.6 Next steps

1. **N-01 · Configure Clerk**
   - Needs: Clerk account API keys.
   - Tasks:
     - Add the keys to `.env.local`.
     - Wrap the app in `<ClerkProvider>` in `app/layout.tsx`.
     - Add `proxy.ts` for route protection.
     - Add sign-in and sign-up pages.
2. **N-02 · Set up the database**
   - Needs: a database choice.
   - Tasks:
     - Choose Prisma 8 RC (current npm `latest`) or Prisma 7.10.1 (previous stable).
     - Add the Prisma client and schema.
     - Add a `prisma.config.ts` in `goldensync-web/`.
     - Connect the database.
3. **N-03 · Apply the design tokens**
   - Map the `DESIGN_RULES.md` tokens into the Tailwind theme in `app/globals.css`.
4. **N-04 · Add tool configs**
   - Add Prettier and Lighthouse CI config files.
5. **N-05 · Move the repo off the USB drive**
   - See ISS-03.
